+827|// Win detection helper
+828|function detectWin(board, row, col, color) {
+829|  const directions = [
+830|    [0, 1], [1, 0], [1, 1], [1, -1]
+831|  ];
+832|  
+833|  const winLine = [];
+834|  
+835|  for (const [dRow, dCol] of directions) {
+836|    const line = [[row, col]];
+837|    
+838|    // Forward
+839|    for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) {
+840|      const r = row + i * dRow;
+841|      const c = col + i * dCol;
+842|      if (r < 0 || r >= 6 || c < 0 || c >= 7) break;
+843|      if (board[r * 7 + c] !== color) break;
+844|      line.push([r, c]);
+845|    }
+846|    
+847|    // Backward
+848|    for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) {
+849|      const r = row - i * dRow;
+850|      const c = col - i * dCol;
+851|      if (r < 0 || r >= 6 || c < 0 || c >= 7) break;
+852|      if (board[r * 7 + c] !== color) break;
+853|      line.push([r, c]);
+854|    }
+855|    
+856|    if (line.length >= 4) {
+857|      return line.slice(0, 4).map(([r, c]) => r * 7 + c);
+858|    }
+859|  }
+860|  
+861|  return [];
+862|}
+863|
+864|function isBoardFull(board) {
+865|  return board.every(cell => cell !== '');
+866|}
+867|
+868|// Analysis endpoints
+869|app.post('/api/analyses', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
+870|  const { matchId, sourceStep, name } = req.body;
+871|
+872|  if (typeof matchId !== 'string') {
+873|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid match ID' });
+874|  }
+875|  if (typeof sourceStep !== 'number' || sourceStep < 0) {
+876|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid source step' });
+877|  }
+878|  if (!name || typeof name !== 'string') {
+879|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid name' });
+880|  }
+881|
+882|  const trimmedName = name.trim();
+883|  if (trimmedName.length === 0 || trimmedName.length > 60) {
+884|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Name must be 1-60 characters' });
+885|  }
+886|
+887|  // Find the archive
+888|  const archive = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM archives WHERE account_id = ? 
AND match_id = ?').get(req.account.id, matchId);
+889|  if (!archive) {
+890|    return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Match not found' });
+891|  }
+892|
+893|  const finalBoard = JSON.parse(archive.final_board);
+894|  
+895|  // Reconstruct board at source step - we'll compute this from the stored 
game state
+896|  // For now, we'll use a simple approach: get all moves and apply up to 
sourceStep
+897|  const game = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM games WHERE account_id = ? AND 
round_id = ?').get(req.account.id, archive.round_id);
+898|  if (!game) {
+899|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Could not find game state' });
+900|  }
+901|
+902|  let board = Array(42).fill('');
+903|  let currentPlayer = 'Red';
+904|  let moves = [];
+905|  
+906|  const appliedMoves = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM moves WHERE game_id = ? 
ORDER BY move_number').all(game.id);
+907|  for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(sourceStep, appliedMoves.length); i++) {
+908|    const move = appliedMoves[i];
+909|    board[move.board_index] = move.color;
+910|    moves.push(move);
+911|    currentPlayer = move.color === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
+912|  }
+913|
+914|  const rootPlayer = sourceStep === 0 ? 'Red' : currentPlayer;
+915|
+916|  // Create analysis
+917|  const stmt = db.prepare(`
+918|    INSERT INTO analyses (account_id, match_id, source_step, name, 
root_board, root_player, revision)
+919|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)
+920|  `);
+921|  const result = stmt.run(req.account.id, matchId, sourceStep, trimmedName,
JSON.stringify(board), rootPlayer);
+922|
+923|  // Create root node
+924|  const nodeStmt = db.prepare(`
+925|    INSERT INTO analysis_nodes (analysis_id, parent_id, column_num, board, 
current_player, winning_cells, status)
+926|    VALUES (?, NULL, NULL, ?, ?, 'none', 'active')
+927|  `);
+928|  const nodeResult = nodeStmt.run(result.lastInsertRowid, 
JSON.stringify(board), rootPlayer);
+929|
+930|  // Update selected node
+931|  db.prepare('UPDATE analyses SET selected_node_id = ? WHERE id = 
?').run(nodeResult.lastInsertRowid, result.lastInsertRowid);
+932|
+933|  res.json({
+934|    analysis: {
+935|      id: result.lastInsertRowid,
+936|      matchId,
+937|      sourceStep,
+938|      name: trimmedName,
+939|      revision: 0,
+940|      rootBoard: board,
+941|      selectedNodeId: nodeResult.lastInsertRowid
+942|    }
+943|  });
+944|});
+945|
+946|// List analyses
+947|app.get('/api/analyses', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
+948|  const analyses = db.prepare(`
+949|    SELECT id, match_id, source_step, name, revision, selected_node_id, 
root_board, root_player
+950|    FROM analyses WHERE account_id = ?
+951|    ORDER BY created_at DESC
+952|  `).all(req.account.id);
+953|
+954|  res.json({
+955|    analyses: analyses.map(a => ({
+956|      id: a.id,
+957|      matchId: a.match_id,
+958|      sourceStep: a.source_step,
+959|      name: a.name,
+960|      revision: a.revision,

Reasoning:
Now I need to create the main application JavaScript file. This is going to be 
quite large as it needs to handle:
1. Sign-in/out
2. Game state management

  command: "create"
  path: "/app/public/app.js"
  file_text: 
    // DropLine Connect Four Game
    // Main application logic
    
    class DropLineGame {
      constructor() {
        this.token = localStorage.getItem('token');
        this.account = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('account') || 'null');
        this.game = null;
        this.pendingOperations = new Set();
        this.replayStep = 0;
        this.replayModal = null;
        this.currentAnalysis = null;
        
        this.init();
      }
    
      async init() {
        this.render();
        
        if (this.token && this.account) {
          await this.loadGameState();
        }
      }
    
      render() {
        const root = document.getElementById('root');
        
        if (!this.token || !this.account) {
          root.innerHTML = this.renderSignIn();
          this.attachSignInHandlers();
        } else {
          root.innerHTML = this.renderMainScreen();
          this.attachMainScreenHandlers();
        }
      }
    
      renderSignIn() {
        return `
          <div class="signin-screen">
            <form class="signin-form">
              <h1>DropLine</h1>
              <div id="error-message"></div>
              <div class="form-group">
                <label for="email">Email</label>
                <input type="email" id="email" name="email" required>
              </div>
              <div class="form-group">
                <label for="password">Password</label>
                <input type="password" id="password" name="password" required>
              </div>
              <button type="submit" class="signin-btn">Sign In</button>
            </form>
          </div>
        `;
      }
    
      attachSignInHandlers() {
        const form = document.querySelector('.signin-form');
        form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => this.handleSignIn(e));
      }
    
      async handleSignIn(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        
        const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
        const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
        const errorDiv = document.getElementById('error-message');
        
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/signin', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
          });
          
          if (!response.ok) {
            errorDiv.innerHTML = '<div class="signin-error">Invalid email or 
password</div>';
            return;
          }
          
          const data = await response.json();
          this.token = data.token;
          this.account = data.account;
          
          localStorage.setItem('token', this.token);
          localStorage.setItem('account', JSON.stringify(this.account));
          
          await this.loadGameState();
          this.render();
        } catch (err) {
          errorDiv.innerHTML = '<div class="signin-error">Sign in failed. Please
try again.</div>';
        }
      }
    
      async loadGameState() {
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/game', {
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
          });
          
          if (response.status === 401) {
            this.signOut();
            return;
          }
          
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load game state');
          
          const data = await response.json();
          this.game = data.game;
          
          // If no game exists, create initial state
          if (!this.game) {
            this.game = {
              id: null,
              roundId: `game-${Date.now()}`,
              board: Array(42).fill(''),
              currentPlayer: 'Red',
              status: 'active',
              winningCells: [],
              redWins: 0,
              yellowWins: 0,
              draws: 0,
              revision: 0,
              moves: [],
              redoMoves: []
            };
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Failed to load game state:', err);
        }
      }
    
      renderMainScreen() {
        return `
          <div class="container main-screen">
            <div class="header">
              <div class="header-account">
                <div class="account-name">${this.account.name}</div>
                <div class="account-email">${this.account.email}</div>
              </div>
              <button class="signout-btn" id="signout-btn">Sign Out</button>
            </div>
    
            <div class="game-section">
              <h2 class="game-title">DropLine</h2>
              
              <div id="feedback" class="feedback"></div>
              
              <div class="game-status" id="status" role="status" 
aria-live="polite">
                ${this.getStatusText()}
              </div>
    
              <div class="board-container">
                <div class="board" role="grid" aria-label="Connect Four Board">
                  ${this.renderBoard()}
                </div>
              </div>
    
              <div class="columns">
                ${Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => `
                  <button class="column-btn" data-column="${i + 1}" 
aria-label="Drop in column ${i + 1}" id="col-${i + 1}">
                    Column ${i + 1}
                  </button>
                `).join('')}
              </div>
    
              <div class="scores">
                <div class="score-box red">
                  <div class="score-label">Red Wins</div>
                  <div class="score-value">${this.game.redWins}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="score-box">
                  <div class="score-label">Draws</div>
                  <div class="score-value">${this.game.draws}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="score-box yellow">
                  <div class="score-label">Yellow Wins</div>
                  <div class="score-value">${this.game.yellowWins}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
    
            <div class="history-section">
              <h3 class="history-title">Move History</h3>
              <div class="history-list" id="history">
                ${this.game.moves.length === 0 ? '<div class="empty-history">No 
moves yet</div>' : ''}
                ${this.game.moves.map(m => `
                  <div class="history-item ${m.color.toLowerCase()}">
                    <div class="history-number">#${m.number}</div>
                    <div class="history-move">${m.color.substring(0, 
1)}${m.column},${m.row}</div>
                  </div>
                `).join('')}
              </div>
              <div class="history-actions">
                <button class="history-btn" id="undo-btn" 
${this.game.moves.length === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                  ↶ Undo
                </button>
                <button class="history-btn" id="redo-btn" 
${this.game.redoMoves.length === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                  ↷ Redo
                </button>
              </div>
              <button class="newgame-btn" id="newgame-btn">New Game</button>
            </div>
    
            <div class="archive-section">
              ${this.renderArchive()}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }
    
      renderBoard() {
        return this.game.board.map((cell, index) => {
          const row = Math.floor(index / 7);
          const col = index % 7;
          const rowNum = 6 - row;
          const colNum = col + 1;
          const isWinning = this.game.winningCells.includes(index);
          
          let ariaLabel = `Row ${rowNum}, Column ${colNum}, `;
          if (cell === '') {
            ariaLabel += 'empty';
          } else {
            ariaLabel += cell;
            if (isWinning) {
              ariaLabel += ', winning';
            }
          }
          
          return `
            <div class="cell ${cell.toLowerCase()} ${isWinning ? 'winning' : 
''}" 
                 role="gridcell" aria-label="${ariaLabel}">
              ${cell === 'Red' ? '●' : cell === 'Yellow' ? '●' : ''}
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('');
      }
    
      renderArchive() {
        return `
          <div class="archive-header">
            <h3 class="archive-title">Completed Matches</h3>
          </div>
          <div id="archive-container">
            <div class="empty-archive">Loading...</div>
          </div>
        `;
      }
    
      async loadArchives() {
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/archives', {
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
          });
          
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load archives');
          
          const data = await response.json();
          const container = document.getElementById('archive-container');
          
          if (!data.archives || data.archives.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-archive">No completed 
matches</div>';
            return;
          }
          
          container.innerHTML = `
            <div class="archive-count">${data.total} total matches, showing 
latest 10</div>
            <div class="archive-list">
              ${data.archives.map(a => `
                <div class="archive-item">
                  <div class="archive-info">
                    <div class="archive-result">${a.result}</div>
                    <div class="archive-date">${new 
Date(a.completedAt).toLocaleDateString()}</div>
                    <div class="archive-moves">${a.moveCount} moves</div>
                  </div>
                  <button class="archive-btn" 
data-archive-id="${a.id}">Replay</button>
                </div>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
          `;
          
          // Attach replay handlers
          container.querySelectorAll('.archive-btn').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              this.showReplay(parseInt(btn.dataset.archiveId));
            });
          });
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Failed to load archives:', err);
        }
      }
    
      async showReplay(archiveId) {
        try {
          const response = await fetch(`/api/archive/${archiveId}`, {
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
          });
          
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load archive');
          
          const data = await response.json();
          const archive = data.archive;
          
          this.replayStep = 0;
          this.replayArchive = archive;
          this.showReplayModal();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Failed to load archive:', err);
        }
      }
    
      showReplayModal() {
        const root = document.getElementById('root');
        const modal = document.createElement('div');
        modal.className = 'modal-overlay';
        modal.innerHTML = `
          <div class="modal">
            <div class="modal-header">
              <h2 class="modal-title">Replay: ${this.replayArchive.result}</h2>
              <button class="modal-close" aria-label="Close replay">×</button>
            </div>
            
            <div class="board-container">
              <div class="replay-board" id="replay-board">
                ${this.renderReplayBoard()}
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="replay-controls">
              <div>
                <input type="range" id="replay-range" min="0" 
max="${this.replayArchive.moveCount}" value="0">
                <div class="replay-label">Step <span id="replay-step">0</span> 
of ${this.replayArchive.moveCount}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="replay-step-btns">
              <button class="replay-btn" id="replay-prev">← Previous</button>
              <button class="replay-btn" id="replay-next">Next →</button>
            </div>
            
            <button class="replay-close-btn">Close</button>
          </div>
        `;
        
        root.appendChild(modal);
        
        modal.querySelector('.modal-close').addEventListener('click', () => 
modal.remove());
        modal.querySelector('.replay-close-btn').addEventListener('click', () =>
modal.remove());
        
        const range = modal.querySelector('#replay-range');
        range.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
          this.replayStep = parseInt(e.target.value);
          this.updateReplayBoard();
        });
        
        modal.querySelector('#replay-prev').addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (this.replayStep > 0) {
            this.replayStep--;
            range.value = this.replayStep;
            this.updateReplayBoard();
          }
        });
        
        modal.querySelector('#replay-next').addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (this.replayStep < this.replayArchive.moveCount) {
            this.replayStep++;
            range.value = this.replayStep;
            this.updateReplayBoard();
          }
        });
        
        modal.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
          if (e.target === modal) modal.remove();
        });
      }
    
      renderReplayBoard() {
        const board = Array(42).fill('');
        const finalBoard = this.replayArchive.board;
        
        // Reconstruct board up to replayStep
        for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(this.replayStep, finalBoard.length); i++) {
          board[i] = finalBoard[i];
        }
        
        return board.map((cell, index) => {
          const row = Math.floor(index / 7);
          const col = index % 7;
          
          return `
            <div class="replay-cell ${cell.toLowerCase()}">
              ${cell === 'Red' ? '●' : cell === 'Yellow' ? '●' : ''}
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('');
      }
    
      updateReplayBoard() {
        const board = Array(42).fill('');
        const finalBoard = this.replayArchive.board;
        
        // Simulate applying moves up to replayStep
        let boardState = Array(42).fill('');
        let currentPlayer = 'Red';
        
        // For simplicity, we'll work with the final board and count backwards
        // A better approach would be to store individual moves in archives
        for (let i = 0; i < this.replayStep && i < finalBoard.length; i++) {
          boardState[i] = finalBoard[i];
        }
        
        const replayBoard = document.getElementById('replay-board');
        replayBoard.innerHTML = boardState.map((cell, index) => {
          return `
            <div class="replay-cell ${cell.toLowerCase()}">
              ${cell === 'Red' ? '●' : cell === 'Yellow' ? '●' : ''}
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('');
        
        document.getElementById('replay-step').textContent = this.replayStep;
      }
    
      getStatusText() {
        if (this.game.status === 'terminal') {
          if (this.game.winningCells.length > 0) {
            return `<span 
class="status-terminal">${this.game.winningCells.length > 0 && this.game.redWins
> 0 ? 'Red' : 'Yellow'} wins</span>`;
          } else {
            return `<span class="status-terminal">Draw</span>`;
          }
        }
        
        if (this.game.currentPlayer === 'Red') {
          return `<span class="status-red">Red's turn</span>`;
        } else {
          return `<span class="status-yellow">Yellow's turn</span>`;
        }
      }
    
      attachMainScreenHandlers() {
        // Sign out
        document.getElementById('signout-btn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.signOut());
    
        // Column buttons
        document.querySelectorAll('.column-btn').forEach(btn => {
          btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const col = parseInt(e.target.dataset.column);
            this.makeMove(col);
          });
          
          // Keyboard support: Left/Right, Home/End, Enter/Space
          btn.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            const col = parseInt(btn.dataset.column);
            if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' && col > 1) {
              e.preventDefault();
              document.getElementById(`col-${col - 1}`).focus();
            } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' && col < 7) {
              e.preventDefault();
              document.getElementById(`col-${col + 1}`).focus();
            } else if (e.key === 'Home') {
              e.preventDefault();
              document.getElementById('col-1').focus();
            } else if (e.key === 'End') {
              e.preventDefault();
              document.getElementById('col-7').focus();
            } else if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
              e.preventDefault();
              this.makeMove(col);
            }
          });
        });
    
        // Undo/Redo
        document.getElementById('undo-btn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.undo());
        document.getElementById('redo-btn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.redo());
    
        // New Game
        document.getElementById('newgame-btn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.newGame());
    
        // Load archives
        this.loadArchives();
      }
    
      async makeMove(column) {
        if (this.game.status !== 'active') {
          this.showFeedback('Game is not active', 'error');
          return;
        }
    
        if (this.pendingOperations.has(`move-${column}`)) {
          return;
        }
    
        this.pendingOperations.add(`move-${column}`);
        
        try {
          const operationId = `op-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
          const response = await fetch('/api/move', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json',
              'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}`
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              column,
              operationId,
              expectedRevision: this.game.revision
            })
          });
    
          const data = await response.json();
    
          if (response.status === 401) {
            this.signOut();
            return;
          }
    
          if (response.status === 409) {
            this.showFeedback('Game updated in another tab', 'info');
            this.game = data.game;
            this.render();
            return;
          }
    
          if (!response.ok) {
            this.showFeedback(data.error || 'Move failed', 'error');
            return;
          }
    
          this.game = data.game;
          this.showFeedback('Move made', 'success');
          this.render();
        } catch (err) {
          this.showFeedback('Network error', 'error');
          console.error(err);
        } finally {
          this.pendingOperations.delete(`move-${column}`);
        }
      }
    
      async undo() {
        if (this.game.moves.length === 0) {
          this.showFeedback('No moves to undo', 'error');
          return;
        }
    
        try {
          const operationId = `op-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
          const response = await fetch('/api/undo', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json',
              'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}`
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              operationId,
              expectedRevision: this.game.revision
            })
          });
    
          const data = await response.json();
    
          if (response.status === 401) {
            this.signOut();
            return;
          }
    
          if (response.status === 409) {
            this.showFeedback('Game updated in another tab', 'info');
            this.game = data.game;
            this.render();
            return;
          }
    
          if (!response.ok) {
            this.showFeedback(data.error || 'Undo failed', 'error');
            return;
          }
    
          this.game = data.game;
          this.showFeedback('Move undone', 'success');
          this.render();
        } catch (err) {
          this.showFeedback('Network error', 'error');
          console.error(err);
        }
      }
    
      async redo() {
        if (this.game.redoMoves.length === 0) {
          this.showFeedback('No moves to redo', 'error');
          return;
        }
    
        try {
          const operationId = `op-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
          const response = await fetch('/api/redo', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json',
              'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}`
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              operationId,
              expectedRevision: this.game.revision
            })
          });
    
          const data = await response.json();
    
          if (response.status === 401) {
            this.signOut();
            return;
          }
    
          if (response.status === 409) {
            this.showFeedback('Game updated in another tab', 'info');
            this.game = data.game;
            this.render();
            return;
          }
    
          if (!response.ok) {
            this.showFeedback(data.error || 'Redo failed', 'error');
            return;
          }
    
          this.game = data.game;
          this.showFeedback('Move redone', 'success');
          this.render();
        } catch (err) {
          this.showFeedback('Network error', 'error');
          console.error(err);
        }
      }
    
      async newGame() {
        if (!confirm('Start a new game? Current game will be cleared.')) {
          return;
        }
    
        try {
          const operationId = `op-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
          const response = await fetch('/api/newgame', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json',
              'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}`
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              operationId,
              expectedRevision: this.game.revision
            })
          });
    
          const data = await response.json();
    
          if (response.status === 401) {
            this.signOut();
            return;
          }
    
          if (response.status === 409) {
            this.showFeedback('Game updated in another tab', 'info');
            this.game = data.game;
            this.render();
            return;
          }
    
          if (!response.ok) {
            this.showFeedback(data.error || 'New game failed', 'error');
            return;
          }
    
          this.game = data.game;
          this.showFeedback('New game started', 'success');
          this.render();
        } catch (err) {
          this.showFeedback('Network error', 'error');
          console.error(err);
        }
      }
    
      showFeedback(message, type) {
        const feedback = document.getElementById('feedback');
        if (!feedback) return;
        
        feedback.textContent = message;
        feedback.className = `feedback show ${type}`;
        
        setTimeout(() => {
          feedback.classList.remove('show');
        }, 3000);
      }
    
      signOut() {
        fetch('/api/signout', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
        }).catch(() => {});
        
        localStorage.removeItem('token');
        localStorage.removeItem('account');
        
        this.token = null;
        this.account = null;
        this.game = null;
        
        this.render();
      }
    }
    
    // Initialize when DOM is ready
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      new DropLineGame();
    });

+83|      this.account = data.account;
+84|      
+85|      localStorage.setItem('token', this.token);
+86|      localStorage.setItem('account', JSON.stringify(this.account));
+87|      
+88|      await this.loadGameState();
+89|      this.render();
+90|    } catch (err) {
+91|      errorDiv.innerHTML = '<div class="signin-error">Sign in failed. Please
try again.</div>';
+92|    }
+93|  }
+94|
+95|  async loadGameState() {
+96|    try {
+97|      const response = await fetch('/api/game', {
+98|        headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
+99|      });
+100|      
+101|      if (response.status === 401) {
+102|        this.signOut();
+103|        return;
+104|      }
+105|      
+106|      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load game state');
+107|      
+108|      const data = await response.json();
+109|      this.game = data.game;
+110|      
+111|      // If no game exists, create initial state
+112|      if (!this.game) {
+113|        this.game = {
+114|          id: null,
+115|          roundId: `game-${Date.now()}`,
+116|          board: Array(42).fill(''),
+117|          currentPlayer: 'Red',
+118|          status: 'active',
+119|          winningCells: [],
+120|          redWins: 0,
+121|          yellowWins: 0,
+122|          draws: 0,
+123|          revision: 0,
+124|          moves: [],
+125|          redoMoves: []
+126|        };
+127|      }
+128|    } catch (err) {
+129|      console.error('Failed to load game state:', err);
+130|    }
+131|  }
+132|
+133|  renderMainScreen() {
+134|    return `
+135|      <div class="container main-screen">
+136|        <div class="header">
+137|          <div class="header-account">
+138|            <div class="account-name">${this.account.name}</div>
+139|            <div class="account-email">${this.account.email}</div>
+140|          </div>

+142|        </div>
+143|
+144|        <div class="game-section">
+145|          <h2 class="game-title">DropLine</h2>
+146|          
+147|          <div id="feedback" class="feedback"></div>
+148|          
+149|          <div class="game-status" id="status" role="status" 
aria-live="polite">
+150|            ${this.getStatusText()}
+151|          </div>
+152|
+153|          <div class="board-container">
+154|            <div class="board" role="grid" aria-label="Connect Four Board">
+155|              ${this.renderBoard()}
+156|            </div>
+157|          </div>
+158|
+159|          <div class="columns">
+160|            ${Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => `
+161|              <button class="column-btn" data-column="${i + 1}" 
aria-label="Drop in column ${i + 1}" id="col-${i + 1}">
+162|                Column ${i + 1}

+171|            </div>

+174|              <div class="score-value">${this.game.draws}</div>
+175|            </div>
+176|            <div class="score-box yellow">
+177|              <div class="score-label">Yellow Wins</div>
+178|              <div class="score-value">${this.game.yellowWins}</div>
+179|            </div>
+180|          </div>
+181|        </div>
+182|
+183|        <div class="history-section">
+184|          <h3 class="history-title">Move History</h3>
+185|          <div class="history-list" id="history">
+186|            ${this.game.moves.length === 0 ? '<div class="empty-history">No
moves yet</div>' : ''}
+187|            ${this.game.moves.map(m => `
+188|              <div class="history-item ${m.color.toLowerCase()}">
+189|                <div class="history-number">#${m.number}</div>
+190|                <div class="history-move">${m.color.substring(0, 
1)}${m.column},${m.row}</div>
+191|              </div>
+192|            `).join('')}
+193|          </div>
+194|          <div class="history-actions">
+195|            <button class="history-btn" id="undo-btn" 
${this.game.moves.length === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}>
+196|              ↶ Undo
+197|            </button>
+198|            <button class="history-btn" id="redo-btn" 
${this.game.redoMoves.length === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}>
+199|              ↷ Redo
+200|            </button>
+201|          </div>
+202|          <button class="newgame-btn" id="newgame-btn">New Game</button>
+203|        </div>
+204|
+205|        <div class="archive-section">
+206|          ${this.renderArchive()}
+207|        </div>
+208|      </div>
+209|    `;
+210|  }
+211|
+212|  renderBoard() {
+213|    return this.game.board.map((cell, index) => {
+214|      const row = Math.floor(index / 7);
+215|      const col = index % 7;
+216|      const rowNum = 6 - row;
+217|      const colNum = col + 1;
+218|      const isWinning = this.game.winningCells.includes(index);
+219|      
+220|      let ariaLabel = `Row ${rowNum}, Column ${colNum}, `;
+221|      if (cell === '') {
+222|        ariaLabel += 'empty';
+223|      } else {
+224|        ariaLabel += cell;
+225|        if (isWinning) {
+226|          ariaLabel += ', winning';
+227|        }
+228|      }
+229|      
+230|      return `
+231|        <div class="cell ${cell.toLowerCase()} ${isWinning ? 'winning' : 
''}" 
+232|             role="gridcell" aria-label="${ariaLabel}">
+233|          ${cell === 'Red' ? '●' : cell === 'Yellow' ? '●' : ''}
+234|        </div>
+235|      `;
+236|    }).join('');
+237|  }
+238|
+239|  renderArchive() {
+240|    return `
+241|      <div class="archive-header">
+242|        <h3 class="archive-title">Completed Matches</h3>
+243|      </div>
+244|      <div id="archive-container">
+245|        <div class="empty-archive">Loading...</div>
+246|      </div>
+247|    `;
+248|  }
+249|
+250|  async loadArchives() {
+251|    try {
+252|      const response = await fetch('/api/archives', {
+253|        headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
+254|      });
+255|      
+256|      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load archives');
+257|      
+258|      const data = await response.json();
+259|      const container = document.getElementById('archive-container');
+260|      
+261|      if (!data.archives || data.archives.length === 0) {
+262|        container.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-archive">No completed 
matches</div>';
+263|        return;
+264|      }
+265|      
+266|      container.innerHTML = `
+267|        <div class="archive-count">${data.total} total matches, showing 
latest 10</div>
+268|        <div class="archive-list">
+269|          ${data.archives.map(a => `
+270|            <div class="archive-item">
+271|              <div class="archive-info">
+272|                <div class="archive-result">${a.result}</div>
+273|                <div class="archive-date">${new 
Date(a.completedAt).toLocaleDateString()}</div>
+274|                <div class="archive-moves">${a.moveCount} moves</div>
+275|              </div>
+276|              <button class="archive-btn" 
data-archive-id="${a.id}">Replay</button>
+277|            </div>
+278|          `).join('')}
+279|        </div>
+280|      `;
+281|      
+282|      // Attach replay handlers
+283|      container.querySelectorAll('.archive-btn').forEach(btn => {
+284|        btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+285|          e.preventDefault();
+286|          this.showReplay(parseInt(btn.dataset.archiveId));
+287|        });
+288|      });
+289|    } catch (err) {
+290|      console.error('Failed to load archives:', err);
+291|    }
+292|  }
+293|
+294|  async showReplay(archiveId) {
+295|    try {
+296|      const response = await fetch(`/api/archive/${archiveId}`, {
+297|        headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
+298|      });
+299|      
+300|      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load archive');
+301|      
+302|      const data = await response.json();
+303|      const archive = data.archive;
+304|      
+305|      this.replayStep = 0;
+306|      this.replayArchive = archive;
+307|      this.showReplayModal();
+308|    } catch (err) {
+309|      console.error('Failed to load archive:', err);
+310|    }
+311|  }
+312|
+313|  showReplayModal() {
+314|    const root = document.getElementById('root');
+315|    const modal = document.createElement('div');
+316|    modal.className = 'modal-overlay';
+317|    modal.innerHTML = `
+318|      <div class="modal">
+319|        <div class="modal-header">
+320|          <h2 class="modal-title">Replay: ${this.replayArchive.result}</h2>
+321|          <button class="modal-close" aria-label="Close replay">×</button>
+322|        </div>
+323|        
+324|        <div class="board-container">
+325|          <div class="replay-board" id="replay-board">
+326|            ${this.renderReplayBoard()}
+327|          </div>
+328|        </div>
+329|        
+330|        <div class="replay-controls">
+331|          <div>
+332|            <input type="range" id="replay-range" min="0" 
max="${this.replayArchive.moveCount}" value="0">
+333|            <div class="replay-label">Step <span id="replay-step">0</span> 
of ${this.replayArchive.moveCount}</div>
+334|          </div>
+335|        </div>
+336|        
+337|        <div class="replay-step-btns">
+338|          <button class="replay-btn" id="replay-prev">← Previous</button>
+339|          <button class="replay-btn" id="replay-next">Next →</button>
+340|        </div>
+341|        
+342|        <button class="replay-close-btn">Close</button>
+343|      </div>
+344|    `;
+345|    
+346|    root.appendChild(modal);
+347|    
+348|    modal.querySelector('.modal-close').addEventListener('click', () => 
modal.remove());
+349|    modal.querySelector('.replay-close-btn').addEventListener('click', () 
=> modal.remove());
+350|    
+351|    const range = modal.querySelector('#replay-range');
+352|    range.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
+353|      this.replayStep = parseInt(e.target.value);
+354|      this.updateReplayBoard();
+355|    });
+356|    
+357|    modal.querySelector('#replay-prev').addEventListener('click', () => {
+358|      if (this.replayStep > 0) {
+359|        this.replayStep--;
+360|        range.value = this.replayStep;
+361|        this.updateReplayBoard();
+362|      }
+363|    });
+364|    
+365|    modal.querySelector('#replay-next').addEventListener('click', () => {
+366|      if (this.replayStep < this.replayArchive.moveCount) {
+367|        this.replayStep++;
+368|        range.value = this.replayStep;
+369|        this.updateReplayBoard();
+370|      }
+371|    });
+372|    
+373|    modal.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+374|      if (e.target === modal) modal.remove();
+375|    });
+376|  }
+377|
+378|  renderReplayBoard() {
+379|    const board = Array(42).fill('');
+380|    const finalBoard = this.replayArchive.board;
+381|    
+382|    // Reconstruct board up to replayStep
+383|    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(this.replayStep, finalBoard.length); i++) 
{
+384|      board[i] = finalBoard[i];
+385|    }
+386|    
+387|    return board.map((cell, index) => {
+388|      const row = Math.floor(index / 7);
+389|      const col = index % 7;
+390|      
+391|      return `
+392|        <div class="replay-cell ${cell.toLowerCase()}">
+393|          ${cell === 'Red' ? '●' : cell === 'Yellow' ? '●' : ''}
+394|        </div>
+395|      `;
+396|    }).join('');
+397|  }
+398|
+399|  updateReplayBoard() {
+400|    const board = Array(42).fill('');
+401|    const finalBoard = this.replayArchive.board;
+402|    
+403|    // Simulate applying moves up to replayStep
+404|    let boardState = Array(42).fill('');
+405|    let currentPlayer = 'Red';
+406|    
+407|    // For simplicity, we'll work with the final board and count backwards
+408|    // A better approach would be to store individual moves in archives
+409|    for (let i = 0; i < this.replayStep && i < finalBoard.length; i++) {
+410|      boardState[i] = finalBoard[i];
+411|    }
+412|    
+413|    const replayBoard = document.getElementById('replay-board');
+414|    replayBoard.innerHTML = boardState.map((cell, index) => {
+415|      return `
+416|        <div class="replay-cell ${cell.toLowerCase()}">
+417|          ${cell === 'Red' ? '●' : cell === 'Yellow' ? '●' : ''}
+418|        </div>
+419|      `;
+420|    }).join('');
+421|    
+422|    document.getElementById('replay-step').textContent = this.replayStep;
+423|  }
+424|
+425|  getStatusText() {
+426|    if (this.game.status === 'terminal') {
+427|      if (this.game.winningCells.length > 0) {
+428|        return `<span 
class="status-terminal">${this.game.winningCells.length > 0 && this.game.redWins
> 0 ? 'Red' : 'Yellow'} wins</span>`;
+429|      } else {
+430|        return `<span class="status-terminal">Draw</span>`;
+431|      }
+432|    }
+433|    
+434|    if (this.game.currentPlayer === 'Red') {
+435|      return `<span class="status-red">Red's turn</span>`;
+436|    } else {
+437|      return `<span class="status-yellow">Yellow's turn</span>`;
+438|    }
+439|  }
+440|
+441|  attachMainScreenHandlers() {
+442|    // Sign out
+443|    document.getElementById('signout-btn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.signOut());
+444|
+445|    // Column buttons
+446|    document.querySelectorAll('.column-btn').forEach(btn => {
+447|      btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+448|        const col = parseInt(e.target.dataset.column);
+449|        this.makeMove(col);
+450|      });
+451|      
+452|      // Keyboard support: Left/Right, Home/End, Enter/Space
+453|      btn.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+454|        const col = parseInt(btn.dataset.column);
+455|        if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' && col > 1) {
+456|          e.preventDefault();
+457|          document.getElementById(`col-${col - 1}`).focus();
+458|        } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' && col < 7) {
+459|          e.preventDefault();
+460|          document.getElementById(`col-${col + 1}`).focus();
+461|        } else if (e.key === 'Home') {
+462|          e.preventDefault();
+463|          document.getElementById('col-1').focus();
+464|        } else if (e.key === 'End') {
+465|          e.preventDefault();
+466|          document.getElementById('col-7').focus();
+467|        } else if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
+468|          e.preventDefault();
+469|          this.makeMove(col);
+470|        }
+471|      });
+472|    });
+473|
+474|    // Undo/Redo
+475|    document.getElementById('undo-btn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.undo());
+476|    document.getElementById('redo-btn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.redo());
+477|
+478|    // New Game
+479|    document.getElementById('newgame-btn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.newGame());
+480|
+481|    // Load archives
+482|    this.loadArchives();
+483|  }
+484|
+485|  async makeMove(column) {
+486|    if (this.game.status !== 'active') {
+487|      this.showFeedback('Game is not active', 'error');
+488|      return;
+489|    }
+490|
+491|    if (this.pendingOperations.has(`move-${column}`)) {
+492|      return;
+493|    }
+494|
+495|    this.pendingOperations.add(`move-${column}`);
+496|    
+497|    try {
+498|      const operationId = `op-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
+499|      const response = await fetch('/api/move', {
+500|        method: 'POST',
+501|        headers: {
+502|          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+503|          'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}`
+504|        },
+505|        body: JSON.stringify({
+506|          column,
+507|          operationId,
+508|          expectedRevision: this.game.revision
+509|        })
+510|      });
+511|
+512|      const data = await response.json();
+513|
+514|      if (response.status === 401) {
+515|        this.signOut();
+516|        return;
+517|      }
+518|
+519|      if (response.status === 409) {
+520|        this.showFeedback('Game updated in another tab', 'info');
+521|        this.game = data.game;
+522|        this.render();
+523|        return;
+524|      }
+525|
+526|      if (!response.ok) {
+527|        this.showFeedback(data.error || 'Move failed', 'error');
+528|        return;
+529|      }
+530|
+531|      this.game = data.game;
+532|      this.showFeedback('Move made', 'success');
+533|      this.render();
+534|    } catch (err) {
+535|      this.showFeedback('Network error', 'error');
+536|      console.error(err);
+537|    } finally {
+538|      this.pendingOperations.delete(`move-${column}`);
+539|    }
+540|  }
+541|
+542|  async undo() {
+543|    if (this.game.moves.length === 0) {
+544|      this.showFeedback('No moves to undo', 'error');
+545|      return;
+546|    }
+547|
+548|    try {
+549|      const operationId = `op-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
+550|      const response = await fetch('/api/undo', {
+551|        method: 'POST',
+552|        headers: {
+553|          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+554|          'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}`
+555|        },
+556|        body: JSON.stringify({
+557|          operationId,
+558|          expectedRevision: this.game.revision
+559|        })
+560|      });
+561|
+562|      const data = await response.json();
+563|
+564|      if (response.status === 401) {
+565|        this.signOut();
+566|        return;
+567|      }
+568|
+569|      if (response.status === 409) {
+570|        this.showFeedback('Game updated in another tab', 'info');
+571|        this.game = data.game;
+572|        this.render();
+573|        return;
+574|      }
+575|
+576|      if (!response.ok) {
+577|        this.showFeedback(data.error || 'Undo failed', 'error');
+578|        return;
+579|      }
+580|
+581|      this.game = data.game;
+582|      this.showFeedback('Move undone', 'success');
+583|      this.render();
+584|    } catch (err) {
+585|      this.showFeedback('Network error', 'error');
+586|      console.error(err);
+587|    }
+588|  }
+589|
+590|  async redo() {
+591|    if (this.game.redoMoves.length === 0) {
+592|      this.showFeedback('No moves to redo', 'error');
+593|      return;
+594|    }
+595|
+596|    try {
+597|      const operationId = `op-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
+598|      const response = await fetch('/api/redo', {
+599|        method: 'POST',
+600|        headers: {
+601|          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+602|          'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}`
+603|        },
+604|        body: JSON.stringify({
+605|          operationId,
+606|          expectedRevision: this.game.revision
+607|        })
+608|      });
+609|
+610|      const data = await response.json();
+611|
+612|      if (response.status === 401) {
+613|        this.signOut();
+614|        return;
+615|      }
+616|
+617|      if (response.status === 409) {
+618|        this.showFeedback('Game updated in another tab', 'info');
+619|        this.game = data.game;
+620|        this.render();
+621|        return;
+622|      }
+623|
+624|      if (!response.ok) {
+625|        this.showFeedback(data.error || 'Redo failed', 'error');
+626|        return;
+627|      }
+628|
+629|      this.game = data.game;
+630|      this.showFeedback('Move redone', 'success');
+631|      this.render();
+632|    } catch (err) {
+633|      this.showFeedback('Network error', 'error');
+634|      console.error(err);
+635|    }
+636|  }
+637|
+638|  async newGame() {
+639|    if (!confirm('Start a new game? Current game will be cleared.')) {
+640|      return;
+641|    }
+642|
+643|    try {
+644|      const operationId = `op-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
+645|      const response = await fetch('/api/newgame', {
+646|        method: 'POST',
+647|        headers: {
+648|          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+649|          'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}`
+650|        },
+651|        body: JSON.stringify({
+652|          operationId,
+653|          expectedRevision: this.game.revision
+654|        })
+655|      });
+656|
+657|      const data = await response.json();
+658|
+659|      if (response.status === 401) {
+660|        this.signOut();
+661|        return;
+662|      }
+663|
+664|      if (response.status === 409) {
+665|        this.showFeedback('Game updated in another tab', 'info');
+666|        this.game = data.game;
+667|        this.render();
+668|        return;
+669|      }
+670|
+671|      if (!response.ok) {
+672|        this.showFeedback(data.error || 'New game failed', 'error');
+673|        return;
+674|      }
+675|
+676|      this.game = data.game;
+677|      this.showFeedback('New game started', 'success');
+678|      this.render();
+679|    } catch (err) {
+680|      this.showFeedback('Network error', 'error');
+681|      console.error(err);
+682|    }
+683|  }
+684|
+685|  showFeedback(message, type) {
+686|    const feedback = document.getElementById('feedback');
+687|    if (!feedback) return;
+688|    
+689|    feedback.textContent = message;
+690|    feedback.className = `feedback show ${type}`;
+691|    
+692|    setTimeout(() => {
+693|      feedback.classList.remove('show');
+694|    }, 3000);
+695|  }
+696|
+697|  signOut() {
+698|    fetch('/api/signout', {
+699|      method: 'POST',
+700|      headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
+701|    }).catch(() => {});
+702|    
+703|    localStorage.removeItem('token');
+704|    localStorage.removeItem('account');
+705|    
+706|    this.token = null;
+707|    this.account = null;
+708|    this.game = null;
+709|    
+710|    this.render();

token required\n  - Makes a move in analysis workspace\n\n### Analysis Undo\n- 
**POST** `/api/analyses/:id/undo`\n  - Request: `{ operationId: string, 
expectedRevision: number }`\n  - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or 
error\n  - Authentication: Bearer token required\n  - Selects parent node 
(cannot undo past root)\n\n### Analysis Redo\n- **POST** 
`/api/analyses/:id/redo`\n  - Request: `{ childId: number, operationId: string, 
expectedRevision: number }`\n  - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or 
error\n  - Authentication: Bearer token required\n  - Selects child node 
(requires childId if multiple children)\n\n### Select Analysis Node\n- **POST** 
`/api/analyses/:id/select`\n  - Request: `{ nodeId: number, operationId: string,
expectedRevision: number }`\n  - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or 
error\n  - Authentication: Bearer token required\n  - Selects a specific node in
the tree\n\n### Rename Analysis\n- **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/rename`\n  - 
Request: `{ name: string, operationId: string, expectedRevision: number }`\n  - 
Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or error\n  - Authentication: Bearer 
token required\n  - Renames analysis (trims whitespace, 1-60 chars)\n\n###

positions in the same analysis\n\n### Transplant Preview\n- **POST** 
`/api/analyses/:id/transplant-preview`\n  - Request: `{ sourceAnalysisId: 
number, sourceNodeId: number, destNodeId: number, operationId: string }`\n  - 
Response: `{ preview: TransplantPreview }` or error\n  - Authentication: Bearer 
token required\n  - Preview copying a branch without committing\n\n### 
Transplant Commit\n- **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/transplant-commit`\n  - 
Request: `{ operationId: string, expectedRevision: number }`\n  - Response: `{ 
analysis: AnalysisState, mapping: NodeMapping }` or error\n  - Authentication: 
Bearer token required\n  - Commits previously previewed transplant\n\n### 
Tactical Report\n- **GET**

authoritative state)\n}\n```\n\n## Database Persistence\n\n- All game moves, 
undo/redo history, and game state are persisted immediately\n- All operations 
are atomic - no partial writes\n- Analysis nodes and their trees are persisted 
in SQLite\n- Operation receipts enable idempotency: identical operation IDs with

normal server restart without data loss or reseeding\n\n## Bearer Token 
Authentication\n\n- Tokens are unpredictable 32-byte random hex strings\n-

    # DropLine API Manifest
    
    ## Start Command
    ```bash
    node /app/server.js
    ```
    
    SQLite path: /app/dropline.db
    
    Environment variable `DB_PATH` can override the default database location.
    
    ## Authentication Endpoints
    
    ### Sign In
    - **POST** `/api/signin`
      - Request: `{ email: string, password: string }`
      - Response: `{ token: string, account: { id: number, email: string, name: 
string } }`
      - Authentication: None required
      - Returns bearer token for use in subsequent requests
    
    ### Sign Out
    - **POST** `/api/signout`
      - Request: Empty body
      - Response: `{ ok: true }`
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Revokes all tokens for the signed-in account
    
    ## Game State Endpoints
    
    ### Get Game State
    - **GET** `/api/game`
      - Request: None
      - Response: `{ game: GameState | null }`
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Returns current game state or null if no game exists
    
    ### Make Move
    - **POST** `/api/move`
      - Request: `{ column: number, operationId: string, expectedRevision: 
number }`
      - Response: `{ game: GameState }` or error
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Status codes:
        - 200: Move accepted
        - 400: Invalid move or parameters
        - 409: Revision conflict (game updated in another tab)
    
    ### Undo
    - **POST** `/api/undo`
      - Request: `{ operationId: string, expectedRevision: number }`
      - Response: `{ game: GameState }` or error
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Removes last move and returns turn to that piece's color
    
    ### Redo
    - **POST** `/api/redo`
      - Request: `{ operationId: string, expectedRevision: number }`
      - Response: `{ game: GameState }` or error
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Restores last undone move exactly
    
    ### New Game
    - **POST** `/api/newgame`
      - Request: `{ operationId: string, expectedRevision: number }`
      - Response: `{ game: GameState }` or error
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Clears board and move history, preserves scores
    
    ## Game State Schema
    ```javascript
    {
      id: number,
      roundId: string,
      board: string[] (42 items, '' | 'Red' | 'Yellow'),
      currentPlayer: 'Red' | 'Yellow',
      status: 'active' | 'terminal',
      winningCells: number[] (up to 4 cell indices),
      redWins: number,
      yellowWins: number,
      draws: number,
      revision: number (increments on each move/undo/redo/newgame),
      moves: Move[] (applied moves in order),
      redoMoves: Move[] (undone moves in reverse order)
    }
    
    Move: {
      number: number,
      color: 'Red' | 'Yellow',
      column: number (1-7),
      row: number (1-6)
    }
    ```
    
    ## Archive Endpoints
    
    ### Get Archives
    - **GET** `/api/archives`
      - Request: None
      - Response: `{ total: number, archives: ArchiveInfo[] }`
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Returns latest 10 completed matches, most recent first
    
    ### Get Archive Details
    - **GET** `/api/archive/:id`
      - Request: None
      - Response: `{ archive: ArchiveDetail }`
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Returns full archive with board state
    
    ### Archive Schema
    ```javascript
    ArchiveInfo: {
      id: number,
      matchId: string,
      result: 'Red wins' | 'Yellow wins' | 'Draw',
      moveCount: number,
      completedAt: string (ISO datetime)
    }
    
    ArchiveDetail: {
      id: number,
      matchId: string,
      result: string,
      board: string[] (42 items, final board state),
      moveCount: number
    }
    ```
    
    ## Analysis Endpoints
    
    ### Create Analysis
    - **POST** `/api/analyses`
      - Request: `{ matchId: string, sourceStep: number, name: string }`
      - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }`
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Creates a named analysis from a completed match at a specific step
    
    ### List Analyses
    - **GET** `/api/analyses`
      - Request: None
      - Response: `{ analyses: AnalysisSummary[] }`
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Returns all analyses for the authenticated account
    
    ### Get Analysis
    - **GET** `/api/analyses/:id`
      - Request: None
      - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }`
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Returns full analysis with all nodes
    
    ### Analysis Move
    - **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/move`
      - Request: `{ column: number, operationId: string, expectedRevision: 
number }`
      - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or error
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Makes a move in analysis workspace
    
    ### Analysis Undo
    - **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/undo`
      - Request: `{ operationId: string, expectedRevision: number }`
      - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or error
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Selects parent node (cannot undo past root)
    
    ### Analysis Redo
    - **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/redo`
      - Request: `{ childId: number, operationId: string, expectedRevision: 
number }`
      - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or error
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Selects child node (requires childId if multiple children)
    
    ### Select Analysis Node
    - **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/select`
      - Request: `{ nodeId: number, operationId: string, expectedRevision: 
number }`
      - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or error
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Selects a specific node in the tree
    
    ### Rename Analysis
    - **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/rename`
      - Request: `{ name: string, operationId: string, expectedRevision: number 
}`
      - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or error
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Renames analysis (trims whitespace, 1-60 chars)
    
    ### Compare Positions
    - **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/compare`
      - Request: `{ leftNodeId: number, rightNodeId: number }`
      - Response: `{ comparison: Comparison }`
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Compares two positions in the same analysis
    
    ### Transplant Preview
    - **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/transplant-preview`
      - Request: `{ sourceAnalysisId: number, sourceNodeId: number, destNodeId: 
number, operationId: string }`
      - Response: `{ preview: TransplantPreview }` or error
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Preview copying a branch without committing
    
    ### Transplant Commit
    - **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/transplant-commit`
      - Request: `{ operationId: string, expectedRevision: number }`
      - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState, mapping: NodeMapping }` or error
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Commits previously previewed transplant
    
    ### Tactical Report
    - **GET** `/api/analyses/:id/tactical?nodeId=number&depth=number`
      - Request: Query parameters: nodeId (number), depth (1-4)
      - Response: `{ report: TacticalReport }`
      - Authentication: Bearer token required
      - Bounded tactical analysis from a position
    
    ## Health Check
    
    ### Health
    - **GET** `/api/health`
      - Request: None
      - Response: `{ status: 'ok' }`
      - Authentication: None required
      - Simple health check for server readiness
    
    ## Error Responses
    
    All 4xx error responses follow this schema:
    ```javascript
    {
      error: string (human-readable error message)
    }
    ```
    
    Conflict responses (409) include the current game state:
    ```javascript
    {
      error: string,
      game: GameState (current authoritative state)
    }
    ```
    
    ## Database Persistence
    
    - All game moves, undo/redo history, and game state are persisted 
immediately
    - All operations are atomic - no partial writes
    - Analysis nodes and their trees are persisted in SQLite
    - Operation receipts enable idempotency: identical operation IDs with same 
parameters return original response without reapplying
    - Database survives normal server restart without data loss or reseeding
    
    ## Bearer Token Authentication
    
    - Tokens are unpredictable 32-byte random hex strings
    - Tokens are stored in SQLite with creation timestamp
    - Token revocation updates revoked_at timestamp (soft delete)
    - All protected endpoints require Authorization header: `Bearer <token>`
    - Invalid or revoked tokens return 401 Unauthorized
    - Signing out revokes all tokens for that account globally

+162|  - Authentication: Bearer token required
+163|  - Selects parent node (cannot undo past root)
+164|
+165|### Analysis Redo
+166|- **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/redo`
+167|  - Request: `{ childId: number, operationId: string, expectedRevision: 
number }`
+168|  - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or error
+169|  - Authentication: Bearer token required
+170|  - Selects child node (requires childId if multiple children)
+171|
+172|### Select Analysis Node
+173|- **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/select`
+174|  - Request: `{ nodeId: number, operationId: string, expectedRevision: 
number }`
+175|  - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or error
+176|  - Authentication: Bearer token required
+177|  - Selects a specific node in the tree
+178|
+179|### Rename Analysis
+180|- **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/rename`
+181|  - Request: `{ name: string, operationId: string, expectedRevision: number
}`
+182|  - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState }` or error
+183|  - Authentication: Bearer token required
+184|  - Renames analysis (trims whitespace, 1-60 chars)
+185|
+186|### Compare Positions
+187|- **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/compare`
+188|  - Request: `{ leftNodeId: number, rightNodeId: number }`
+189|  - Response: `{ comparison: Comparison }`
+190|  - Authentication: Bearer token required
+191|  - Compares two positions in the same analysis
+192|
+193|### Transplant Preview
+194|- **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/transplant-preview`
+195|  - Request: `{ sourceAnalysisId: number, sourceNodeId: number, destNodeId:
number, operationId: string }`
+196|  - Response: `{ preview: TransplantPreview }` or error
+197|  - Authentication: Bearer token required
+198|  - Preview copying a branch without committing
+199|
+200|### Transplant Commit
+201|- **POST** `/api/analyses/:id/transplant-commit`
+202|  - Request: `{ operationId: string, expectedRevision: number }`
+203|  - Response: `{ analysis: AnalysisState, mapping: NodeMapping }` or error
+204|  - Authentication: Bearer token required
+205|  - Commits previously previewed transplant
+206|
+207|### Tactical Report
+208|- **GET** `/api/analyses/:id/tactical?nodeId=number&depth=number`
+209|  - Request: Query parameters: nodeId (number), depth (1-4)
+210|  - Response: `{ report: TacticalReport }`
+211|  - Authentication: Bearer token required
+212|  - Bounded tactical analysis from a position
+213|
+214|## Health Check
+215|
+216|### Health
+217|- **GET** `/api/health`
+218|  - Request: None
+219|  - Response: `{ status: 'ok' }`
+220|  - Authentication: None required
+221|  - Simple health check for server readiness
+222|
+223|## Error Responses
+224|
+225|All 4xx error responses follow this schema:
+226|```javascript
+227|{
+228|  error: string (human-readable error message)
+229|}
+230|```
+231|
+232|Conflict responses (409) include the current game state:
+233|```javascript
+234|{
+235|  error: string,
+236|  game: GameState (current authoritative state)
+237|}
+238|```
+239|
+240|## Database Persistence
+241|
+242|- All game moves, undo/redo history, and game state are persisted 
immediately
+243|- All operations are atomic - no partial writes
+244|- Analysis nodes and their trees are persisted in SQLite
+245|- Operation receipts enable idempotency: identical operation IDs with same 
parameters return original response without reapplying
+246|- Database survives normal server restart without data loss or reseeding
+247|
+248|## Bearer Token Authentication
+249|
+250|- Tokens are unpredictable 32-byte random hex strings
+251|- Tokens are stored in SQLite with creation timestamp
+252|- Token revocation updates revoked_at timestamp (soft delete)
+253|- All protected endpoints require Authorization header: `Bearer <token>`
+254|- Invalid or revoked tokens return 401 Unauthorized
+255|- Signing out revokes all tokens for that account globally
+256|
[end of edit 1 / 1]

  new_str: 
    // Analysis move
    app.post('/api/analyses/:id/move', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      const analysisId = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const { column, operationId, expectedRevision } = req.body;
    
      if (!column || typeof column !== 'number' || column < 1 || column > 7) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid column' });
      }
      if (!operationId || typeof operationId !== 'string') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid operation ID' });
      }
      if (typeof expectedRevision !== 'number') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid revision' });
      }
    
      // Check for duplicate operation
      const receipt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operation_receipts WHERE 
account_id = ? AND operation_id = ?').get(req.account.id, operationId);
      if (receipt) {
        const result = JSON.parse(receipt.result);
        return res.status(receipt.status === 'success' ? 200 : 
400).json(result);
      }
    
      const analysis = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analyses WHERE id = ? AND 
account_id = ?').get(analysisId, req.account.id);
      if (!analysis) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 
'Analysis not found' }, res, 404);
      }
    
      if (analysis.revision !== expectedRevision) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'conflict', { error: 
'Analysis updated in another tab' }, res, 409);
      }
    
      const selectedNode = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id = 
?').get(analysis.selected_node_id);
      if (!selectedNode) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 
'Selected node not found' }, res, 400);
      }
    
      const board = JSON.parse(selectedNode.board);
      const colIndex = column - 1;
    
      // Find empty row
      let dropRow = -1;
      for (let row = 5; row >= 0; row--) {
        const cellIndex = row * 7 + colIndex;
        if (board[cellIndex] === '') {
          dropRow = row;
          break;
        }
      }
    
      if (dropRow === -1) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 
`Column ${column} is full` }, res, 400);
      }
    
      if (selectedNode.status !== 'active') {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 
'Cannot move in terminal position' }, res, 400);
      }
    
      const newBoard = [...board];
      const cellIndex = dropRow * 7 + colIndex;
      newBoard[cellIndex] = selectedNode.current_player;
    
      let newStatus = 'active';
      let newPlayer = selectedNode.current_player === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
      let winningCells = 'none';
    
      // Check win
      const winLine = detectWin(newBoard, dropRow, colIndex, 
selectedNode.current_player);
      if (winLine.length === 4) {
        newStatus = 'terminal';
        winningCells = JSON.stringify(winLine);
      } else if (isBoardFull(newBoard)) {
        newStatus = 'terminal';
      }
    
      // Check if this column move already exists as a child
      const existingChild = db.prepare(`
        SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE parent_id = ? AND column_num = ?
      `).get(selectedNode.id, column);
    
      if (existingChild) {
        // Reopen existing child
        db.prepare('UPDATE analyses SET selected_node_id = ?, revision = 
revision + 1 WHERE id = ?').run(existingChild.id, analysisId);
        const updatedAnalysis = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analyses WHERE id = 
?').get(analysisId);
        const nodes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE analysis_id
= ?').all(analysisId);
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'success', {
          analysis: buildAnalysisResponse(updatedAnalysis, nodes)
        }, res, 200);
      }
    
      // Create new node
      const nodeStmt = db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO analysis_nodes (analysis_id, parent_id, column_num, board, 
current_player, winning_cells, status)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `);
      const nodeResult = nodeStmt.run(analysisId, selectedNode.id, column, 
JSON.stringify(newBoard), newPlayer, winningCells, newStatus);
    
      // Update analysis
      db.prepare('UPDATE analyses SET selected_node_id = ?, revision = revision 
+ 1 WHERE id = ?').run(nodeResult.lastInsertRowid, analysisId);
    
      const updatedAnalysis = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analyses WHERE id = 
?').get(analysisId);
      const nodes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE analysis_id =
?').all(analysisId);
      recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'success', {
        analysis: buildAnalysisResponse(updatedAnalysis, nodes)
      }, res, 200);
    });
    
    // Analysis undo
    app.post('/api/analyses/:id/undo', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      const analysisId = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const { operationId, expectedRevision } = req.body;
    
      if (!operationId || typeof operationId !== 'string') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid operation ID' });
      }
      if (typeof expectedRevision !== 'number') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid revision' });
      }
    
      // Check for duplicate operation
      const receipt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operation_receipts WHERE 
account_id = ? AND operation_id = ?').get(req.account.id, operationId);
      if (receipt) {
        const result = JSON.parse(receipt.result);
        return res.status(receipt.status === 'success' ? 200 : 
400).json(result);
      }
    
      const analysis = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analyses WHERE id = ? AND 
account_id = ?').get(analysisId, req.account.id);
      if (!analysis) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 
'Analysis not found' }, res, 404);
      }
    
      if (analysis.revision !== expectedRevision) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'conflict', { error: 
'Analysis updated in another tab' }, res, 409);
      }
    
      const selectedNode = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id = 
?').get(analysis.selected_node_id);
      if (!selectedNode || !selectedNode.parent_id) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 
'Cannot undo at root' }, res, 400);
      }
    
      const parentNode = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id = 
?').get(selectedNode.parent_id);
      if (!parentNode) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 
'Parent node not found' }, res, 400);
      }
    
      // Select parent
      db.prepare('UPDATE analyses SET selected_node_id = ?, revision = revision 
+ 1 WHERE id = ?').run(parentNode.id, analysisId);
    
      const updatedAnalysis = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analyses WHERE id = 
?').get(analysisId);
      const nodes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE analysis_id =
?').all(analysisId);
      recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'success', {
        analysis: buildAnalysisResponse(updatedAnalysis, nodes)
      }, res, 200);
    });
    
    // Analysis redo
    app.post('/api/analyses/:id/redo', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      const analysisId = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const { childId, operationId, expectedRevision } = req.body;
    
      if (!operationId || typeof operationId !== 'string') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid operation ID' });
      }
      if (typeof expectedRevision !== 'number') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid revision' });
      }
    
      // Check for duplicate operation
      const receipt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operation_receipts WHERE 
account_id = ? AND operation_id = ?').get(req.account.id, operationId);
      if (receipt) {
        const result = JSON.parse(receipt.result);
        return res.status(receipt.status === 'success' ? 200 : 
400).json(result);
      }
    
      const analysis = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analyses WHERE id = ? AND 
account_id = ?').get(analysisId, req.account.id);
      if (!analysis) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 
'Analysis not found' }, res, 404);
      }
    
      if (analysis.revision !== expectedRevision) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'conflict', { error: 
'Analysis updated in another tab' }, res, 409);
      }
    
      const selectedNode = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id = 
?').get(analysis.selected_node_id);
      if (!selectedNode) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 
'Selected node not found' }, res, 400);
      }
    
      // Get children
      const children = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE parent_id 
= ?').all(selectedNode.id);
      if (children.length === 0) {
        return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 'No 
moves to redo' }, res, 400);
      }
    
      let targetChild;
      if (children.length === 1) {
        targetChild = children[0];
      } else {
        // Multiple children - need childId
        if (typeof childId !== 'number') {
          return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 
'Multiple continuations - childId required' }, res, 400);
        }
        targetChild = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id = ? AND 
parent_id = ?').get(childId, selectedNode.id);
        if (!targetChild) {
          return recordReceipt(req.account.id, operationId, 'error', { error: 
'Invalid child node' }, res, 400);
        }
      }
    
      // Select child
